## [E4CM](https://www.youtube.com/@Exercise4CheatMeals)

### [Watermelon Sorbet](https://youtu.be/MUU71EH9URw)
Tags: sorbet, watermelon, dairy-free, low-calorie, 3-ingredient

**Ingredients:**
- **330g** fresh watermelon juice (blended/pureed)
- **120g** water
- **20g** erythritol
- **0.5g** salt

**Steps:**
1. Blend watermelon into juice.
2. Add all to pint, mix with immersion blender 30 sec.
3. Freeze 24h.
4. Sorbet setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin.
6. Re-Spin again if needed (total: Sorbet + 2 Re-Spins).

---

### [Cantaloupe Sorbet](https://youtu.be/MUU71EH9URw)
Tags: sorbet, cantaloupe, dairy-free, low-calorie, 3-ingredient

**Ingredients:**
- **300g** fresh cantaloupe juice (blended/pureed)
- **160g** water
- **20g** erythritol
- **0.5g** salt

**Steps:**
1. Blend cantaloupe into juice.
2. Add all to pint, mix 30 sec.
3. Freeze 24h.
4. Sorbet setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin twice.

---

### [Strawberry Sorbet](https://youtu.be/MUU71EH9URw)
Tags: sorbet, strawberry, dairy-free, low-calorie, 3-ingredient

**Ingredients:**
- **300g** fresh strawberries (rinsed, tops removed)
- **130g** water
- **20g** erythritol
- **0.1g** sucralose
- **0.5g** salt

**Steps:**
1. Add strawberries to pint on scale.
2. Add water, erythritol, salt.
3. Immersion blender 60-90 sec until fully pureed.
4. Freeze 24h.
5. Sorbet setting.
6. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin once (pectin gives great body naturally).

---

### [Raspberry Sorbet](https://youtu.be/e25PNWmd3yQ)
Tags: sorbet, raspberry, dairy-free, low-calorie

**Ingredients:**
- **230g** fresh raspberries (rinsed)
- **210g** water
- **40g** erythritol
- **0.5g** salt

**Steps:**
1. Add all to high-sided container.
2. Immersion blender 90 sec.
3. Strain through sieve into pint (controls seeds — keep half or none).
4. Freeze 24h.
5. Sorbet setting.
6. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin 1-2x.

---

### [Grapefruit Sorbet](https://youtu.be/e25PNWmd3yQ)
Tags: sorbet, grapefruit, dairy-free, low-calorie, citrus

**Ingredients:**
- **220g** grapefruit juice (freshly squeezed, strained)
- **3g** grapefruit zest (about half a large grapefruit)
- **220g** water
- **50g** erythritol
- **0.5g** salt
- **0.5g** xanthan gum (optional — keeps grapefruit evenly suspended)

**Steps:**
1. Zest half a grapefruit into container.
2. Squeeze juice, strain out seeds/pith. Avoid pith at all costs (very bitter).
3. Add 220g juice, 220g water, erythritol, salt.
4. If using xanthan, combine dry first then add while blending.
5. Immersion blender 90 sec. Pour into pint.
6. Freeze 24h.
7. Sorbet setting.
8. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin 1-2x.

---

### [Peach Sorbet](https://youtu.be/e25PNWmd3yQ)
Tags: sorbet, peach, dairy-free, low-calorie

**Ingredients:**
- **300g** fresh peaches (white preferred, pitted)
- **140g** water
- **30g** erythritol
- **0.5g** salt

**Steps:**
1. Halve and pit peaches. Add to container on scale until 300g.
2. Add water, erythritol, salt.
3. Immersion blender until crushed, then 90 sec more.
4. Pour into pint, freeze 24h.
5. Sorbet setting.
6. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin until desired consistency.

### [Chocolate Ice Cream #1 — Basic (Almond Milk + Cow's Milk Blend)](https://youtu.be/YyNNWmWGtI4)
Tags: chocolate, low-calorie, simple

**Base:**
- **160g** 1% milk → 🟢 **160g** Marata 1.5% milk
- **80g** unsweetened almond milk
- **2.1g** vanilla extract

**Dry mix:**
- **50g** erythritol
- **8g** cocoa powder
- **1g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, almond milk, vanilla to pint.
2. Combine dry ingredients in a small bowl.
3. Blend into wet with immersion blender ~30 sec, then 90 sec more.
4. Freeze 24h.
5. Lite Ice Cream setting.
6. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon.
7. Mix-In (pint consistency) or Re-Spin (soft serve).

---

### [Chocolate Ice Cream #2 — All Cow's Milk (Improved Texture)](https://youtu.be/YyNNWmWGtI4)
Tags: chocolate, improved-texture, low-calorie

**Base:**
- **440g** 1% milk → 🟢 **440g** Marata 1.5% milk
- **2.1g** vanilla extract

**Dry mix:**
- **50g** Swerve 🟢
  - **30g** erythritol
  - **20g** xylitol
  - **0.05g**/pinch sucralose
- **8g** cocoa powder
- **1g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Steps:**
1. Blend all with immersion blender 90 sec.
2. Freeze 24h.
3. Lite Ice Cream setting.
4. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon.
5. Mix-In (pint consistency) or Re-Spin (soft serve).

### [Chocolate Ice Cream #3 — Ultimate (Egg Yolk + Melted Chocolate)](https://youtu.be/YyNNWmWGtI4)
Tags: chocolate, premium, egg-yolk, melted-chocolate, best

**Base:**
- **410g** 1% milk → 🟢 **410g** Marata 1.5% milk
- **1** egg yolk
- **2.1g** vanilla extract

**Dry mix:**
- **50g** Swerve 🟢
  - **30g** erythritol
  - **20g** xylitol
  - **0.05g**/pinch sucralose
- **10g** cocoa powder
- **1g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Chocolate:**
- **14g** semisweet chocolate

**Steps:**
1. Warm milk to 27-32°C.
2. Melt chocolate, pour into warm milk, blend.
3. Add dry ingredients, blend.
4. Strain into pint. Wait 10 min.
5. Freeze **36 hours**.
6. Lite Ice Cream setting.
7. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Mix-In.

### [Reese's Puffs Ice Cream — Cereal Edition 2](https://youtu.be/QTtkus690oo)
Tags: cereal, peanut-butter, low-calorie

**Base:**
- **160g** fat-free milk → 🟢 **160g** Marata 1.5% milk
- **280g** unsweetened almond milk
- **4.2g** vanilla extract

**Dry mix:**
- **45g** Swerve 🟢
  - **27g** erythritol
  - **18g** xylitol
  - **0.05g**/pinch sucralose
- **1.5g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum
- **8g** PBfit / powdered PB → 🟡 **4g** peanut butter, blended into the wet ingredients instead (higher fat, heavier — won't disperse as dry powder; blend thoroughly)

**Mix-in:**
- **8g** Reese's Puffs → 🟡 any peanut butter cereal, or skip cereal and drizzle 5g melted PB on top

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, almond milk, vanilla, and 4g peanut butter to pint.
2. Combine erythritol, xylitol, salt, xanthan. Blend into wet, mix 90 seconds.
3. Freeze 24h.
4. Ice Cream setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin.
6. Make hole, add cereal, Mix-In.

### [Cherry Garcia Protein Ice Cream](https://youtu.be/ILEOkNrHdek)
Tags: cherry-garcia, protein, egg-yolk, mix-ins, premium

**Base:**
- **20g** cherry juice (from jar)
- **380g** 2% Fairlife high protein milk 🟡 (slightly richer/heavier pint, less "light" feel)
  - **375g** Marata 1.5%
  - **8g** whey protein
  - **10g** glycerin
- **2** egg yolks
- **4g** vanilla extract

**Dry mix:**
- **16g** PEScience whey/casein blend → 🟢 **16g** whey protein (E4CM says blends over-thicken; whey alone = slightly softer, still excellent)
- **40g** Swerve 🟢
  - **24g** erythritol
  - **16g** xylitol
  - **0.05g**/pinch sucralose
- **0.5g** salt
- **0.8g** xanthan gum

**Mix-ins:**
- bing cherries (5) → 🟡 5 dark sweet cherries, stems removed, pressed dry in paper towels (any dark sweet cherry; avoid sour/vyssino)
- **15g** Hershey's dark chocolate → 🟢 **15g** any dark chocolate, chopped small

**Steps:**
1. Add cherry juice, milk, whey, glycerin, egg yolks, vanilla to high-sided container.
2. Combine dry. Blend into wet, mix 90 seconds. Pour into pint, freeze 24h.
3. Prep mix-ins: press cherries dry, chop chocolate.
4. **Sorbet** setting (thickest consistency).
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon.
6. Alternate layers of chocolate and cherries. Mix-In.

---

### [Oreo Protein Ice Cream — The Secrets to Perfect](https://youtu.be/HbvidbBT-iA)
Tags: oreo, cookies-and-cream, protein, premium

**Base:**
- **1** Oreo (blended into base)
- **410g** Fairlife fat-free milk 🟡 (slightly richer/heavier pint, less "light" feel)
  - **385g** Marata 1.5%
  - **12g** whey protein
  - **15g** glycerin
- **4.2g** vanilla extract

**Dry mix:**
- **~30g** cookies & cream whey protein 🟡 (less cookie flavor in the base — extra Oreo compensates)
  - **30g** whey protein
  - **2g** vanilla extract
  - 1 extra Oreo blended into base
- **10g** erythritol
- **4g** black cocoa powder → 🟡 **4g** cocoa powder + 0.5g baking soda
- **0.5g** salt
- **0.5g** xanthan gum

**Mix-ins (milk-dunked, frozen):**
- **2** Oreos (dunked in milk 15-20 sec, bagged, frozen alongside pint)

**Topping:**
- **1** Oreo (chopped)

**Steps:**
1. Add 2 Oreos (1 per original + 1 to cover protein flavor swap), milk, whey, glycerin, vanilla to high-sided container.
2. Combine dry. Blend into wet with immersion blender, 90 seconds.
3. Pour into pint, freeze 24h. Dunk + freeze mix-in Oreos alongside.
4. **Sorbet** setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon.
6. Make hole near **EDGE** (not center). Add frozen Oreo halves. Cover, Mix-In.
7. Top with chopped dry Oreo.

### [Mint Chocolate Chip Protein Ice Cream](https://youtu.be/ZkWjvlVDZRI)
Tags: mint-chocolate-chip, protein, mix-ins
🔴 **Requires peppermint extract — buy or skip.**

**Chocolate prep (make ahead — yields ~4 pints):**
- **113.5g** semi-sweet chocolate, Ghirardelli → 🟢 **113.5g** any dark chocolate
- **7g** peanut oil → 🟢 **7g** coconut oil
- Double boil, whisk in oil. Spread on parchment 1/8" thick. Freeze. Chop into chunks.

**Base:**
- **430g** 1% Fairlife high protein milk 🟡 (slightly richer/heavier pint, less "light" feel)
  - **415g** Marata 1.5%
  - **12g** whey protein
  - **10g** glycerin
- **4.2g** vanilla extract
- green food coloring (8 drops) → 🟢 skip (cosmetic only)
- 12 drops McCormick / 4 drops LorAnn's 🔴 BUY peppermint extract

**Dry mix:**
- **32.4g** vanilla whey protein, Gorilla Mind → 🟢 **32.4g** whey protein + 2g extra vanilla extract
- **20g** erythritol
- **0.5g** salt
- **0.5g** xanthan gum

**Mix-in:**
- **30g** frozen chocolate chunks (from prep)

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, whey, glycerin, vanilla, peppermint to high-sided container.
2. Combine dry. Blend into wet, 90 seconds. Pour into pint, freeze 24h.
3. **Sorbet** setting.
4. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon.
5. Make hole, add 30g frozen chocolate. Cover, Mix-In.

### [Cookie Dough Protein Ice Cream](https://youtu.be/ZkWjvlVDZRI)
Tags: cookie-dough, protein, mix-ins, premium, egg

**Cookie Dough (make 24h ahead, refrigerate):**
- *Dry:*
  - **90g** flour
  - **48.6g** vanilla whey protein → 🟢 **48.6g** whey protein
  - **1.5g** salt
- *Wet:*
  - **21g** butter (melted)
  - 1 egg
  - 1 egg yolk
  - **4.2g** vanilla extract
  - **24g** erythritol
  - **24g** brown sugar erythritol 🟡 (less toffee/molasses depth in the dough)
  - **24g** erythritol
  - **3g** honey
- **21g** *Fold in:* chocolate chips
- Mix wet, add dry in thirds, fold in chips. Refrigerate 24h.

**Ice Cream Base:**
- **430g** 2% Fairlife high protein milk 🟡 (slightly richer/heavier pint, less "light" feel)
  - **420g** Marata 1.5%
  - **10g** whey protein
  - **10g** glycerin
- **4.2g** vanilla extract

**Dry mix:**
- **32.4g** vanilla whey protein → 🟢 **32.4g** whey protein + 2g extra vanilla extract
- **10g** erythritol
- **0.5g** salt
- **0.5g** xanthan gum

**Mix-in:**
- **50g** cookie dough (from above)

**Steps:**
1. Make cookie dough 24h in advance.
2. Add milk, whey, glycerin, vanilla to high-sided container.
3. Combine dry. Blend into wet, 90 seconds. Pour into pint, freeze 24h.
4. **Sorbet** setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon.
6. Make 1-inch hole, add 50g cookie dough. Cover, Mix-In.

### [Vanilla Bean Chocolate Chip Protein Ice Cream](https://youtu.be/ZkWjvlVDZRI)
Tags: vanilla-bean, chocolate-chip, protein, egg-yolk, premium

**Chocolate prep (make ahead — yields ~4 pints):**
- **113g** semi-sweet chocolate, Ghirardelli → 🟢 **113g** any dark chocolate
- **7g** peanut oil → 🟢 **7g** coconut oil
- Double boil, spread on parchment 1/8–1/4" thick. Freeze. Chop.

**Base:**
- **410g** 2% Fairlife high protein milk 🟡 (slightly richer/heavier pint, less "light" feel)
  - **400g** Marata 1.5%
  - **8g** whey protein
  - **10g** glycerin
- **1** egg yolk
- vanilla bean 1/2 (seeds scraped) → 🟡 use 15g vanilla extract total instead of 4.2g (buy beans — vanilla is the star of this recipe)
- **4.2g** vanilla extract

**Dry mix:**
- **16.2g** vanilla whey protein → 🟢 **16.2g** whey protein (vanilla already loaded from bean/extract)
- **8g** fat-free milk powder 🟡 (slightly less body/creaminess)
  - **5g** extra whey protein
  - **5g** inulin
- **20g** erythritol
- **0.5g** salt
- **0.7g** xanthan gum

**Mix-in:**
- **26g** frozen chocolate chunks (from prep)

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, whey, glycerin, egg yolk, vanilla bean seeds + extract to high-sided container.
2. Combine dry. Blend into wet with immersion blender, 90 seconds.
3. Pour into pint, let foam settle 10 min. Freeze 24h.
4. **Lite Ice Cream** setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon.
6. Make hole. Grab chocolate from freezer ONLY NOW. Push toward bottom. Cover, Mix-In.

### [Cream Pie Protein Ice Cream](https://youtu.be/z6_YxcMwvHQ)
Tags: cream-pie, cinnamon, protein, low-calorie
🔴 **Requires skim milk powder (20g) — buy or skip.**

**Base:**
- **440g** Fairlife fat-free milk 🟡 (slightly richer/heavier pint, less "light" feel)
  - **425g** Marata 1.5%
  - **12g** whey protein
  - **15g** glycerin
- **20g** brown sugar erythritol 🟡 (less toffee/molasses depth)
  - **20g** erythritol
  - **3g** honey
  - drop Manis Milk Cream flavor
- **4.2g** vanilla extract

**Dry mix:**
- **20.3g** vanilla whey protein → 🟢 **20.3g** whey protein + 2g extra vanilla extract
- **20g** fat-free milk powder 🔴 (structural at this amount — buy SMP or skip recipe)
- **8g** cinnamon
- **0.5g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Mix-in (frozen alongside pint):**
- 1 Oatmeal Creme Pie → 🟡 any cream-filled sandwich cookie, frozen

**Topping:**
- 1/3 Oatmeal Creme Pie → 🟡 sandwich cookie, chopped

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, whey, glycerin, sweetener, vanilla to high-sided container.
2. Combine dry (incl. SMP). Blend into wet, 90 seconds. Pour into pint, freeze 24h. Freeze cookie alongside.
3. **Lite Ice Cream** setting.
4. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon.
5. Make hole near **SIDE**. Insert frozen cookie. Cover, Mix-In.
6. Top with chopped cookie pieces.

### [Fudge Brownie Protein Ice Cream](https://youtu.be/z6_YxcMwvHQ)
Tags: fudge-brownie, chocolate, protein, egg-yolk, premium

**Brownie (make ahead):**
- 1 *Wet:* 55g Fairlife 2% milk → 🟢 **55g** Marata 1.5% milk | 28g peanut oil → 🟢 **28g** coconut oil | egg
- zero sugar brownie mix (1 box) 🟡 (denser, less fudgy than commercial mix)
  - **120g** flour
  - **50g** cocoa
  - **80g** erythritol
  - **1g** salt
  - **1g** baking powder
- Mix wet, fold in dry. Spread in 8x8 pan. Bake 175°C, 26-30 min. Cool, chop, freeze.

**Ice Cream Base:**
- **410g** 1% Fairlife high protein milk 🟡 (slightly richer/heavier pint, less "light" feel)
  - **385g** Marata 1.5%
  - **12g** whey protein
  - **15g** glycerin
- **1** egg yolk
- **2.1g** vanilla extract
- coffee extract (6 drops) → 🟢 **1g** instant coffee dissolved in 2g warm water

**Dry mix:**
- **27.8g** chocolate whey protein 🟢
  - **27.8g** whey protein
  - **5g** extra cocoa
  - Manis chocolate flavor drops
- **20g** erythritol
- **12g** cocoa powder (+5g from protein swap = 17g total)
- **0.5g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Mix-in:**
- **44g** frozen brownie bites (in hole) + 12g (side/topping)

**Steps:**
1. Make brownies ahead. Cool, chop, bag, freeze.
2. Add milk, whey, glycerin, egg yolk, vanilla, coffee to high-sided container.
3. Combine dry + chocolate flavor drops. Blend into wet, 90 seconds. Pour into pint, freeze 24h.
4. **Lite Ice Cream** setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon.
6. Make hole near **SIDE**. Add 44g brownie bites, push toward bottom. 12g on side.
7. Cover, Mix-In. Top with extra brownie bites.

### Raspberry Cheesecake Topped Protein Ice Cream
Tags: cheesecake, raspberry, white chocolate, protein

**Base:**
- **400g** Fat Free High Protein Milk 🟡 (slightly richer/heavier pint, less "light" feel)
  - **385g** Marata 1.5%
  - **12g** whey protein
  - **15g** glycerin
- **22g** cream cheese
- **40-45g** Monk Fruit sweetener 🟢
  - **27g** erythritol
  - **18g** xylitol
  - **0.05g**/pinch sucralose
- **5g** vanilla bean paste → 🟢 **5g** vanilla extract
- **0.5g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Mix-ins/Toppings:**
- **28g** frozen raspberries (must be frozen — no extra liquid)
- **14g** Teddy Grahams → 🟡 **14g** digestive biscuits, broken into small pieces (less sweet bite, missing honey-graham flavor)
- **15g** white chocolate chips
- **2g** coconut oil

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, whey, glycerin, cream cheese, sweeteners, vanilla, salt, xanthan to pint.
2. Blend with immersion blender 90 sec.
3. Freeze 24h.
4. 1-2 hours before processing, put biscuit pieces in the freezer so they don't get pulverized by Mix-In.
5. Ice Cream setting.
6. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if powdery.
7. Make a hole down to the bottom. Add frozen biscuit pieces (save 1-2 to crumble on top) and frozen raspberries.
8. Mix-In.
9. Melt white chocolate chips + coconut oil in microwave (30 sec increments until smooth).
10. Pour chocolate mixture over ice cream. Crumble extra biscuit pieces on top.

### Tiramisu Topped Protein Ice Cream
Tags: tiramisu, coffee, mascarpone, protein

**Base:**
- **400g** Fat Free High Protein Milk 🟡 (slightly richer/heavier pint, less "light" feel)
  - **385g** Marata 1.5%
  - **12g** whey protein
  - **15g** glycerin
- **40g** Monk Fruit sweetener 🟢
  - **24g** erythritol
  - **16g** xylitol
  - **0.05g**/pinch sucralose
- **15g** mascarpone
- **5g** vanilla bean paste → 🟢 **5g** vanilla extract
- **0.5g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Mix-ins/Toppings:**
- **10g** HighKey Vanilla Wafers → 🟢 **10g** lady fingers or any vanilla wafers
- **10g** chocolate covered espresso beans (save 1, chop finely for topping)
- **15g** chocolate sauce

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, whey, glycerin, sweeteners, mascarpone, vanilla, salt, xanthan to pint.
2. Blend with immersion blender 90 sec.
3. Freeze 24h.
4. Lite Ice Cream setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if powdery.
6. Make a hole. Add espresso beans and wafers.
7. Mix-In.
8. Top with chocolate sauce and finely chopped espresso bean.

### Pumpkin Cheesecake Protein Ice Cream
Tags: pumpkin, cheesecake, graham cracker, protein

**Base:**
- **400g** Fat Free High Protein Milk 🟡 (slightly richer/heavier pint, less "light" feel)
  - **385g** Marata 1.5%
  - **12g** whey protein
  - **15g** glycerin
- **40-45g** Monk Fruit sweetener 🟢
  - **27g** erythritol
  - **18g** xylitol
  - **0.05g**/pinch sucralose
- **60g** canned pumpkin
- **30g** pumpkin cream cheese → 🟢 **30g** cream cheese + dash cinnamon + dash pumpkin pie spice
- **2.5g** vanilla bean paste → 🟢 **2.5g** vanilla extract
- **2g** pumpkin pie spice 🟢
  - **1g** cinnamon
  - **0.5g** nutmeg
  - **0.25g** allspice
  - **0.25g** ginger
- **0.5g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Toppings:**
- graham cracker (1/2 sheet) → 🟡 **~7g** digestive biscuits, crushed (less sweet, missing honey-graham flavor)

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, whey, glycerin, sweeteners, pumpkin, cream cheese, vanilla, spices, salt, xanthan to pint.
2. Blend with immersion blender 90 sec.
3. Freeze 24h.
4. Lite Ice Cream setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if powdery.
6. Top with crumbled biscuits.

### One Love Protein Ice Cream

8. Top with crumbled biscuits and drizzle caramel syrup.

### Caramel Chocolate Cheesecake Protein Ice Cream
Tags: caramel, chocolate, cheesecake, oreo, protein

  - **24g** erythritol
  - **16g** xylitol
  - **0.05g**/pinch sucralose
- **7g** Jello cheesecake pudding mix → 🟡 **7g** any instant pudding mix (Dr. Oetker), or 15g cream cheese + 2g cornstarch (less custardy body)
- **0.5g** salt

**Mix-ins/Toppings:**
- Oreo Thins (2, cream removed) → 🟢 2 Oreos, halved
- **30g** cheesecake bites, coated in ~2g crushed digestive biscuit

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, whey, glycerin, caramel syrup, sweeteners, pudding mix (or cream cheese + cornstarch), salt to pint.
2. Blend with immersion blender 90 sec.
3. Freeze 24h.
4. Lite Ice Cream setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if powdery.
6. Make a hole. Add Oreos.
7. Mix-In.
8. Top with cheesecake pieces.

### Cookie Dough Protein Ice Cream (FPF)
Tags: cookie dough, protein

**Base:**
- **350-400g** Vanilla Protein Shake 🟡 (less vanilla depth than pre-made shake)
  - **375g** Marata 1.5%
  - **30g** whey protein
  - **15g** glycerin
  - **5g** vanilla extract
- **40g** sweetener (bumped from 25g — shake had built-in sweetener)
  - **24g** erythritol
  - **16g** xylitol
  - **0.05g**/pinch sucralose
- **2.5g** vanilla bean paste → 🟢 **2.5g** vanilla extract
- **1g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Toppings:**
- Fuul Snacks Cookie Dough (1/2 package) → 🟡 **~25g** any cookie dough pieces (less protein, more calories)

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, whey, glycerin, vanilla, sweeteners, salt, xanthan to pint.
2. Blend with immersion blender 90 sec.
3. Freeze 24h.
4. Lite Ice Cream setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if powdery.
6. Top with cookie dough pieces.

### Cherry Garcia Protein Ice Cream (FPF)
Tags: cherry, chocolate, protein

**Base:**
- **350-400g** Vanilla Protein Shake 🟡 (less vanilla depth than pre-made shake)
  - **375g** Marata 1.5%
  - **30g** whey protein
  - **15g** glycerin
  - **5g** vanilla extract
- **100g** cherries (fresh or frozen)
- Monk Fruit sweetener (35g → bumped to 45g — shake had built-in sweetener) 🟢
  - **27g** erythritol
  - **18g** xylitol
  - **0.05g**/pinch sucralose
- **1g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Mix-ins:**
- **15g** chocolate chips + 2g coconut oil (melt, pour into hole)

**Steps:**
1. Add cherries to pint. Cover with sweeteners, salt, xanthan.
2. Let sit a few minutes to macerate.
3. Add milk, whey, glycerin, vanilla on top.
4. Shake pint to combine. Freeze 24h.
5. Lite Ice Cream setting.
6. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if powdery.
7. Make a hole. Pour in melted chocolate.
8. Mix-In.

### Shamrock Oreo McFlurry Protein Ice Cream
Tags: mint, oreo, shamrock, mcflurry, protein
🔴 **Requires peppermint extract — buy or skip.**

**Base:**
- **400g** Fairlife fat-free milk 🟡 (slightly richer pint, less protein-dense)
  - **385g** Marata 1.5%
  - **15g** whey protein
- 40-45g Monk Fruit sweetener (reduced to 38g SweEX — Marata adds ~8g lactose sweetness; lower total for firmer pint) 🟢
  - **20g** erythritol
  - **18g** xylitol
  - **0.15g** sucralose (compensates reduced sugar-alcohol sweetness)
- **22g** inulin (body/solids — compensates low MSNF)
- **5g** vanilla extract 🟢
- **0.6g** peppermint extract (1/8 tsp) 🔴 BUY
- **0.5g**/pinch salt
- **1.5g** xanthan gum
- **1g** guar gum
- **1g** CMC
- **1.5g** soy lecithin

**Mix-ins:**
- 5 Oreo Thins (cream removed) → 🟢 3 Oreos, cream removed (3 regular ≈ 5 Thins by cookie mass)

**Scoopulator:** [low-fat-ice-cream](https://scoopulator.app/calc?ingredients=user-milk-fresh-marata-15-4d71c8:385,user-hsn-whey-protein-concentrate-984009:15,erythritol:20,xylitol:18,usdafndds-2710263:0.15,pure-vanilla-extract:5,usdaff-746775:0.5,xanthan-gum:1.5,guar-gum:1,sodium-carboxymethyl-cellulose-cmc:1,usdabranded-soy-lecithin:1.5,inulin:22,usdafndds-2707920:15&target=low-fat-ice-cream) — PAC 31 | Serv. -13.5°C 🟢 | Sweet 8.28% 🔴 | Solids 27.41% 🟡 | Milk Fat 1.19% 🔴 | Sugars 13.44% 🔴 | MSNF 2.93% 🔴 | Stab 0.72% 🟢 | Emul 0.31% 🟢

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, whey, sweeteners, inulin, vanilla, peppermint, salt, xanthan, guar, CMC, soy lecithin to pint.
2. Blend with immersion blender 90 sec.
3. Freeze 24h.
4. Lite Ice Cream setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if powdery.
6. Make a hole to the bottom with butter knife. Fill with Oreo pieces.
7. Mix-In.

- **5g** chopped pecans
- **15g** chocolate chips + 2g coconut oil (melt, pour into hole)

**Steps:**
1. Add milk, whey, glycerin, sweeteners, vanilla, salt, xanthan to pint.
2. Blend with immersion blender 90 sec.
3. Freeze 24h.
4. Lite Ice Cream setting.
5. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if powdery.
6. Make a hole. Pour in melted chocolate.
7. Mix-In.
8. Top with pecans and drizzle caramel.

## [ice-creamery](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery)

### Almond Buttermilk
Tags: almond, amaretto, buttermilk, scoopable, low-sugar

**Prep (heat to 70°C):**
- **150g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **40g** almond butter (creamy)
- **15g** skim milk powder
- **2g** GMS → 🟢 **2g** soy lecithin

**Wet:**
- **350g** buttermilk 1%
- **10g** glycerin
- **25g** Amaretto 28%

**Dry:**
- **24g** erythritol
- **16g** xylitol
- Salty Stability (15g):
  - **11.0g** inulin
  - **1.8g** soy lecithin
  - **0.9g** CMC
  - **0.6g** guar gum
  - **0.5g** salt
  - **0.2g** xanthan gum

**Fill to MAX:**
- **~73g** buttermilk 1%
- Flavor drops Cookies & Cream (~10 drops) → 🟡 skip *(not available)*

**Mix-ins:**
- **25g** dark chocolate 70%, chopped
- **10g** almond slivers
- **14g** amarettini (~10 pcs)

**Topping (optional):**
- **5g** molasses

**Steps:**
1. Heat 150g soy milk to 70°C. Add almond butter, SMP, and soy lecithin. Blend with immersion blender until smooth.
2. Add buttermilk, glycerin, Amaretto.
3. Combine dry ingredients separately, then pour into pint and blend on full speed, 1-2 min until thickeners hydrate.
4. Add remaining buttermilk (~73g to MAX line) and vanilla extract. Stir.
5. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
6. Lite Ice Cream setting.
7. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.
8. Make a hole to the bottom. Add chocolate, almonds, and amarettini in layers. Mix-In.
9. Drizzle molasses on top if desired.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 689 kcal | 26g fat | 33g protein | 101g carbs

**PAC:** 31

### Almond Milk Mix (Premix)
Tags: premix, almond-milk-base, low-sugar

*Premix of dry ingredients for almond milk bases. Yields ten 50g servings (500g total). Store in airtight container.*

**Dry:**
- **200g** erythritol
- **155g** xylitol
- **100g** inulin
- **20g** salt
- **20g** tara gum → 🟡 **20g** guar gum (similar thickening, slightly different mouthfeel — tara is smoother)
- **5g** xanthan gum

**Steps:**
1. Weigh all dry ingredients into a large jar with a secure lid.
2. Shake vigorously until evenly combined.
3. Store in an airtight container (~1000ml).
4. Use 50g per Deluxe pint base.

**Nutrition (per 50g serving):** 68 kcal | 0g fat | 0g protein | 38g carbs

### Apple Milk Crème
Tags: apple, cinnamon, high-protein, scoopable, low-fat, vegan

**Prep:**
- **75g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **30g** hemp hearts → 🟡 **30g** ground almonds (higher fat, less protein — blends similarly for body)

**Wet:**
- **350g** apple sauce (no added sugar)
- **125g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **15g** glycerin

**Dry:**
- **25g** erythritol
- **25g** soy protein isolate → 🟡 **25g** whey protein (no longer vegan, slightly different water-binding)
- **15g** inulin
- **15g** xylitol
- **2g** cinnamon
- **2g** GMS → 🟢 **2g** soy lecithin
- **2g** CMC
- **2g** salt

**Fill to MAX:**
- **~37g** water
- Flavor drops Caramel + Vanilla → 🟡 Manis milk cream flavor (2-3 drops) + 1g vanilla extract (approximate caramel/vanilla accent)

**Steps:**
1. Blend ground almonds and 75g soy milk in pint to a smooth puree.
2. Add apple sauce, remaining 125g soy milk, glycerin.
3. Combine dry ingredients separately, then blend into wet on full speed, 1-2 min.
4. Add water to MAX line and flavor/extract. Stir.
5. Rest in fridge for 2 hours.
6. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
7. Frozen Yogurt setting.
8. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.

**Mix-in options:** 20g almond slivers, 10g raisins

**Nutrition (per pint):** 678 kcal | 21g fat | 40g protein | 117g carbs

**PAC:** 31

### Berry Punch (Deluxe)
Tags: sorbet, fruit, boozy, draft, vegan, dairy-free

**Source:** ice-creamery — Berry Punch (Deluxe) · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Berry%20Punch%20(Deluxe))

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

Vibrant ruby-red sorbet spiked with spiced berry warmth. Bold & tangy flavor with clove-cinnamon notes. Vegan & dairy-free.

**Wet:**
- **300g** fruit juice blend (cherry+grape or apple+cherry+prune)
- **170g** Bärgløgg 11% → 🟡 **170g** red wine (dry, ~12%) + **1g** cinnamon + **0.3g** ground cloves *(loses gløgg's specific spiced berry depth; wine adds more tannin)*
- **170g** water (cold)

**Dry:**
- **20g** inulin
- **15g** Ultratex (waxy maize starch E1442) → 🟡 **10g** inulin + **0.3g** xanthan gum *(less thickening power, slightly thinner body)*
- **3g** gum arabic (E414) → 🟡 skip *(not available; xanthan above partially compensates)*
- **1g** citric acid
- **1g** xanthan gum
- **10 sweetener tablets** → 🟢 **8g** erythritol + **0.05g** sucralose *(~40g sugar equivalent)*

**Adjust sweetness:**
- 1 drop strawberry flavor drops (MAX 2 drops/pint)

**Steps:**
1. Add wet ingredients to empty Creami tub.
2. Mix dry ingredients separately.
3. Pour into tub and QUICKLY immersion blend on full speed.
4. Let blender run 1-2 min to hydrate thickeners.
5. Add flavor drops, stir.
6. Rest in fridge a few hours or overnight.
7. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
8. Sorbet setting.
9. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 455 kcal | 1.5g fat | 1.7g protein | 69g carbs

**PAC:** 31

⚠️ GI check: Inulin = 20g + 10g (Ultratex swap) = 30g (AT limit). Xylitol = 0g. Gums = 1g xanthan + 0.3g xanthan = 1.3g (under 2g). Erythritol = 8g (1.2% of ~680g).

### Butter Pecan
Tags: butter, pecan, vanilla, scoopable, low-sugar, cooked-base

**Prep:**
- **45g** pecan nuts
- **15g** butter (unsalted)
- **0.75g** salt

**Wet:**
- **400g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **25g** cream cheese 23%
- **10g** glycerin
- **10g** Jamaica rum 43%
- **3g** molasses

**Dry:**
- **24g** erythritol
- **16g** xylitol
- **25g** skim milk powder
- Salty Stability (15g):
  - **11.0g** inulin
  - **1.8g** soy lecithin
  - **0.9g** CMC
  - **0.6g** guar gum
  - **0.5g** salt
  - **0.2g** xanthan gum
- **5g** vanilla bean powder → 🟡 **6g** vanilla extract (add with wet; loses bean specks and depth)

**Fill to MAX:**
- **~86g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- Flavor drops Vanilla (1 drop) → 🟡 skip (covered by vanilla extract above)

**Steps:**
1. Roast pecans in a dry saucepan over medium heat ~5 min until fragrant, stirring occasionally. Set aside.
2. Brown the butter with salt in the same pan until golden. Add pecans, toss. Refrigerate overnight. Reserve 5g browned butter in the pan.
3. Pour wet ingredients into the pan with reserved butter. Heat to ~50°C.
4. Whisk in premixed dry ingredients until dissolved. Let cool slightly, then transfer to pint.
5. Add remaining soy milk (~86g to MAX line) and vanilla extract. Stir.
6. Cool in fridge. Stir once more before freezing.
7. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
8. Sorbet setting.
9. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.
10. Warm the butter-pecan mixture briefly in microwave until butter is liquid. Add to base and quickly start Mix-In before butter re-solidifies.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 907 kcal | 59g fat | 29g protein | 86g carbs

**PAC:** 30

  - **1.2g** soy lecithin
  - **0.6g** CMC
  - **0.4g** guar gum
  - **0.3g** salt
  - **0.13g** xanthan gum

**Fill to MAX:**
- Flavor drops Vanilla (6 drops) → 🟡 **2g** vanilla extract

**Mix-ins (choose one):**
- **30g** 1️⃣ dark chocolate shavings
- **50g** 2️⃣ dark chocolate 85%, melted with 8g olive oil — pour over flattened ice cream, close lid, start Mix-In immediately

**Steps:**
1. Add wet ingredients to pint.
2. Combine dry ingredients separately, then pour into pint and blend on full speed, 1-2 min until thickeners hydrate.
3. Add vanilla extract. Stir.
4. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
5. Sorbet setting.
6. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.
7. For shavings: add to a hole and Mix-In. For melted chocolate: microwave 15s at 600W, stir, 15s more, stir. Pour over flattened ice cream, close lid, Mix-In immediately.
8. Refreeze at least 1 hour before serving.

**Nutrition (per pint, base only):** 656 kcal | 14g fat | 47g protein | 104g carbs

**PAC:** 31

### Buttermilk Vanilla
Tags: buttermilk, vanilla, high-protein, low-sugar, scoopable

**Wet:**
- **500g** buttermilk 1%
- **10g** glycerin
- **6g** vanilla extract

**Dry:**
- **27g** erythritol
- **18g** xylitol
- **20g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **20g** whey protein
- **10g** Ultratex / waxy maize starch → 🟡 **10g** corn starch (heat part of base to activate — corn starch doesn't cold-swell like Ultratex)
- **3g** vanilla bean powder → 🟡 **4g** vanilla extract (total vanilla extract in recipe: 10g)
- **1g** salt

**Fill to MAX:**
- **75g** cream 32%
- Flavor drops Vanilla (3 drops) → 🟡 skip (covered by vanilla extract above)

**Steps:**
1. Heat ~200g of the buttermilk to 70°C.
2. Whisk in premixed dry ingredients (including corn starch) until dissolved and thickened.
3. Pour into pint. Add remaining 300g buttermilk, glycerin, and vanilla extract.
4. Blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min.
5. Add cream to MAX line. Stir.
6. Rest in fridge a few hours or overnight for better flavor and gum hydration.
7. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
8. Lite Ice Cream setting.
9. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 640 kcal | 27g fat | 35g protein | 89g carbs

### Butterscotch
Tags: butterscotch, vanilla, butter, scoopable, cooked-base

**Prep (butterscotch sauce — heat to 150°C):**
- **50g** trehalose → 🟡 **25g** sugar (sugar is ~2x as sweet as trehalose — 25g approximates same sweetness. Caramelizes slightly differently.)
- **40g** butter (unsalted)
- **20g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **10g** xylitol
- **7g** molasses
- **0.3g** salt

**Wet:**
- **350g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **50g** butter (unsalted)
- **10g** glycerin

**Dry:**
- **25g** erythritol
- **25g** skim milk powder
- Salty Stability (15g):
  - **11.0g** inulin
  - **1.8g** soy lecithin
  - **0.9g** CMC
  - **0.6g** guar gum
  - **0.5g** salt
  - **0.2g** xanthan gum
- **4g** vanilla bean powder → 🟡 **5g** vanilla extract (add with wet; loses bean specks)

**Fill to MAX:**
- **~74g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- Flavor drops Vanilla (1 drop) → 🟡 skip (covered by vanilla extract above)

**Topping (optional):**
- **15g** candied pecans or walnuts

**Steps:**
1. Brown the 40g butter in a saucepan. Whisk in sugar, xylitol, 20g soy milk, molasses, and salt.
2. Heat to ~150°C for a firm butterscotch texture.
3. Add the wet ingredients (350g soy milk, 50g butter, glycerin) on low heat and melt together.
4. Whisk in premixed dry ingredients until dissolved.
5. Let cool slightly, then transfer to pint.
6. Add remaining soy milk (~74g to MAX line) and vanilla extract. Stir.
7. Cool fully in fridge before freezing. Stir once more.
8. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
9. Sorbet setting.
10. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 1200 kcal | 82g fat | 23g protein | 125g carbs

**PAC:** 30

**Wet:**
- **200g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **20g** glycerin
- **15g** Cointreau 40% (or any orange liqueur)
- **10g** soy sauce

**Dry:**
- **25g** erythritol
- **17g** xylitol
- **15g** skim milk powder
- **15g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **15g** whey protein
- Salty Stability (10g):
  - **7.3g** inulin
  - **1.2g** soy lecithin
  - **0.6g** CMC
  - **0.4g** guar gum
  - **0.3g** salt
  - **0.13g** xanthan gum

6. Add remaining soy milk (~78g to MAX line) and vanilla extract. Stir.
7. Rest in fridge a few hours or overnight.
8. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.

Tags: watermelon, fruit, sorbet, cream, scoopable

**Wet:**
- **520g** watermelon (blended & strained)
- **20g** glycerin
- **10g** lemon juice

**Dry:**
- **21g** erythritol
- **14g** xylitol
- **10g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **10g** whey protein
- **2g** GMS → 🟢 **2g** soy lecithin
- **1.75g** CMC
- **0.75g** xanthan gum
- **1.5g** salt
- **1g** beet root powder (optional, for color)

**Fill to MAX:**
- **80g** cream 32%
- Flavor drops Peach/Maracuja (5 drops) → 🟡 skip (no peach flavor available; watermelon provides the main flavor)

**Steps:**
1. Scoop pulp from a watermelon, blend, and strain through a mesh.
2. Add wet ingredients to pint.
3. Combine dry ingredients separately, then pour into pint and blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min until thickeners hydrate.
4. Add cream (~80g to MAX line). Stir.
5. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
6. Sorbet setting.
7. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 589 kcal | 27g fat | 13g protein | 100g carbs

**PAC:** 32

### Crème Caramel
Tags: caramel, quark, high-protein, low-fat, low-sugar, scoopable

Creamy, a bit too sweet, nice and strong caramel flavor.

**Wet:**
- **300g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **240g** Topfen/Quark 0.6% → 🟡 **240g** Greek strained yogurt 0-2% (tangier than quark, but functionally similar)
- **50g** caramel syrup (low-sugar)
- **20g** glycerin

**Dry:**
- **27g** erythritol
- **18g** xylitol
- **15g** inulin
- **5g** waxy maize starch / Ultratex → 🟡 **5g** corn starch (heat ~100g of the soy milk to dissolve corn starch first; it doesn't cold-swell like Ultratex)
- **1g** salt

**Fill to MAX:**
- Flavor drops Caramel (3 drops) → 🟡 Manis milk cream flavor (2 drops, MAX 2/pint)
- Flavor drops Vanilla (1 drop) → 🟡 **1g** vanilla extract

**Mix-ins:**
- **40g** medjool dates (pitted, chopped)

**Topping (optional):**
- **10g** caramel syrup (low-sugar)

**Steps:**
1. Heat ~100g of the soy milk to dissolve corn starch. Let cool slightly.
2. Add remaining wet ingredients and cooled corn starch mixture to pint.
3. Combine remaining dry ingredients separately, then pour into pint and blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min until thickeners hydrate.
4. Add vanilla extract and milk cream flavor. Stir.
5. Rest in fridge a few hours or overnight.
6. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
7. Lite Ice Cream setting.
8. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.
9. Make a hole to the bottom. Add chopped dates in layers. Mix-In.

**Nutrition (per pint, base only):** 513 kcal | 6g fat | 38g protein | 106g carbs

**PAC:** 32

### Dark Temptation
Tags: chocolate, rum-raisin, pecan, coffee, scoopable, low-sugar

**Prep (bloom cocoa — boiling water):**
- **200g** water (boiling)
- **50g** cocoa powder
- **50g** dark chocolate 85%
- **5g** instant coffee
- **30g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **30g** whey protein

**Wet:**
- **200g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **15g** glycerin
- **30g** rum 43% (drained from soaking mix-ins)
- **10g** soy sauce

**Dry:**
- **27g** erythritol
- **18g** xylitol
- Salty Stability (15g):
  - **11.0g** inulin
  - **1.8g** soy lecithin
  - **0.9g** CMC
  - **0.6g** guar gum
  - **0.5g** salt
  - **0.2g** xanthan gum

**Fill to MAX:**
- **~70g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- Flavor drops Vanilla (10 drops) → 🟡 **4g** vanilla extract

**Mix-ins (soak overnight in rum):**
- **15g** pecan nuts, coarsely chopped
- **15g** sultanas/raisins
- **45g** total rum 43% (mix-ins absorb ~15g; drain remaining ~30g for the base)

**Steps:**
1. Soak chopped pecans and raisins overnight in 45g rum in a closed container.
2. Mix cocoa, coffee, and whey protein in a bowl.
3. Pour boiling water over the mixture to bloom cocoa. Whisk smooth.
4. Break chocolate into pieces and melt into the hot paste. Let cool slightly.
5. Drain rum from soaked mix-ins (~30g). Add to pint with other wet ingredients and chocolate paste.
6. Combine dry ingredients separately, then pour into pint and blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min until thickeners hydrate.
7. Add remaining soy milk (~70g to MAX line) and vanilla extract. Stir.
8. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
9. Frozen Yogurt setting.
10. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.
11. Make a hole to the bottom. Fold in soaked pecans and raisins manually with a chopstick.

**Nutrition (per pint, incl. mix-ins):** 1127 kcal | 48g fat | 54g protein | 112g carbs

**PAC:** 27

### DC's Blank Slate
Tags: vanilla, cream, cooked-base, simple, scoopable

> Dana Cree's Philadelphia-style base. Rich, classic, traditional.

**Prep:**
- **15g** water (cold)
- **6g** corn starch

**Wet:**
- **240g** whole milk 3.5%
- **230g** cream 32%
- **30g** light corn syrup → 🟡 **30g** glucose syrup, rice syrup, or maple syrup (any liquid sweetener works)

**Dry:**
- **90g** sugar
- **15g** skim milk powder
- **1g** salt

**Steps:**
1. Mix dry ingredients in a small container.
2. In a saucepan, bring cream, milk, and syrup to a full rolling boil while whisking.
3. Whisk in dry ingredients. Simmer 2 minutes while whisking.
4. Mix corn starch and cold water to a smooth slurry. Whisk into the base, cook 1 minute more.
5. Chill quickly in an ice bath.
6. Let base age in fridge for a few hours or overnight.
7. Transfer to pint. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
8. Ice Cream setting.
9. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 1412 kcal | 82g fat | 19g protein | 151g carbs

**PAC:** 24

### Dulce de Leche
Tags: caramel, dates, almond-butter, vegan-origin, high-protein, low-fat, low-sugar, scoopable

> Originally vegan — soy protein → whey swap removes vegan status.

**Prep (heat soy milk to ~50°C):**
- **100g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **40g** medjool dates (pitted) (2 dates)
- **20g** almond butter (creamy)
- **11g** erythritol
- **7g** xylitol
- **2g** GMS → 🟢 **2g** soy lecithin

**Wet:**
- **15g** glycerin
- **10g** brandy or vodka 40%
- **350g** soy milk (unsweetened)

**Dry:**
- **9g** erythritol
- **6g** xylitol
- **15g** inulin
- **15g** soy protein isolate → 🟡 **15g** whey protein (no longer vegan)
- **1g** salt
- **0.7g** CMC
- **0.3g** xanthan gum

**Fill to MAX:**
- **~78g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- Flavor drops Caramel (4 drops) → 🟡 Manis milk cream flavor (3-5 drops)

**Steps:**
1. Cut dates in half, ensure pitted.
2. Mix lecithin with prep erythritol + xylitol in a small bowl.
3. Heat 100g soy milk to ~50°C in the microwave.
4. Blend dates, heated milk, almond butter, and prep sweetener+lecithin to a smooth liquid.
5. Add wet ingredients (glycerin, brandy, 350g soy milk) to pint with the date blend.
6. Combine dry ingredients separately, then pour into pint and blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min until thickeners hydrate.
7. Add remaining soy milk (~78g to MAX line) and milk cream flavor. Stir.
8. Let base cool in fridge for 1-2 hours. Stir to remove foam.
9. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
10. Lite Ice Cream setting.
11. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 614 kcal | 19g fat | 34g protein | 100g carbs

**PAC:** 32

### Fruity FroYo (Deluxe)
Tags: frozen-yogurt, fruit, hi-protein, low-fat, scoopable

**Source:** ice-creamery — Fruity FroYo (Deluxe) · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Fruity%20FroYo%20(Deluxe))

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

Light, protein-enriched frozen yogurt from yogurt and fresh/frozen fruit. Gentle sweetness, smooth scoopable texture.

**Wet:**
- **500g** yogurt 3.5% → 🟡 **500g** Marata 2% strained yogurt *(thicker, less fat — may need extra milk to reach MAX)*
- **100g** strawberries (fresh or frozen)
- **15g** glycerin
- **10g** brandy or vodka 40%

**Dry:**
- SweEX (35g):
  - **21g** erythritol
  - **14g** xylitol
- **10g** skim milk powder
- **10g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **10g** whey protein
- Salty Stability (10g):
  - **7.3g** inulin
  - **1.2g** soy lecithin
  - **0.6g** CMC
  - **0.4g** guar gum
  - **0.3g** salt
  - **0.13g** xanthan gum

**Fill to MAX:**
- Flavor drops Vanilla (4 drops) → 🟡 **2g** vanilla extract

**Optional fruit swaps (replace the 100g strawberries):**
- 100g blueberries (48kcal, 9g sugar)
- 100g kiwi (52kcal, 9g sugar)
- 100g cherries (61kcal, 10g sugar)
- 100g mango (62kcal, 13g sugar)
- 100g pineapple in juice (68kcal, 15g sugar)

**Steps:**
1. Add wet ingredients to empty Creami tub.
2. Mix dry ingredients separately.
3. Pour into tub and QUICKLY immersion blend on full speed.
4. Let blender run 1-2 min to hydrate thickeners.
5. Add vanilla extract. Stir.
6. Rest in fridge a few hours or overnight.
7. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
8. Lite Ice Cream setting.
9. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Ice Cream for 2nd spin.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 608 kcal | 19g fat | 36g protein | 94g carbs

**PAC:** 32

⚠️ GI check: Inulin = 7.3g (from SS). Xylitol = 14g. Gums = 0.6g CMC + 0.4g guar + 0.13g xanthan = 1.13g. Erythritol = 21g (3.0% of 690g). ALL SAFE.

### Fruit Powder Mix
Tags: fruit-powder, multi-flavor, high-protein, low-fat, low-sugar, scoopable, premix

> Premix template — make the dry mix in bulk (x5), then use 93g per tub with wet ingredients.
> Use any fruit or root powder (strawberry, ube, lingonberry, etc.).

**Premix (per tub, 93g — or make x5 batch of 463g):**
- **30g** erythritol
- **15g** inulin
- **15g** skim milk powder
- **15g** whey protein Vanilla → 🟡 **15g** whey protein (unflavored) + 2g vanilla extract in base
- **15g** xylitol
- **1.5g** CMC
- **0.5g** guar gum
- **0.5g** salt
- **0.15g** xanthan gum

**Wet:**
- **400g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **100g** cottage cheese 4%
- **15g** glycerin
- **10g** brandy or vodka 40%

**Dry:**
- **25g** fruit powder of choice (e.g. strawberry, lingonberry, ube)

**Fill to MAX:**
- **~77g** water

**Steps:**
1. If using premix batch: measure 93g from your container. Otherwise weigh premix ingredients individually.
2. Add wet ingredients and vanilla extract to pint.
3. Add premix and fruit powder. Blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min until thickeners hydrate.
4. Add water (~77g to MAX line). Stir.
5. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
6. Lite Ice Cream setting.
7. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 578 kcal | 12g fat | 43g protein | 97g carbs

**PAC:** 32

### GaMe On

- Flavor drops Peach (5 drops) → 🟡 skip (no peach flavor available; melon provides the main flavor)

2. Add melon puree and wet ingredients to pint.
3. Combine dry ingredients separately, then pour into pint and blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min until thickeners hydrate.
4. Add remaining soy milk (~29g to MAX line). Stir.

- **200g** Topfen/Quark 14.4% → 🟡 **200g** Greek strained yogurt 10% (tangy and thick, functionally similar for cheesecake)
- **15g** glycerin
- **15g** lemon juice

**Dry:**

  - **9.2g** inulin
  - **0.92g** CMC

  - **0.32g** salt

- Flavor drops Cookies & Cream (10 drops) → 🟡 skip (no cookies & cream flavor available)

- **20g** sultanas/raisins (chopped)

### Grapple Sorbet
Tags: apple, grape, sorbet, vegan, dairy-free, low-fat, scoopable, light

**Wet:**
- **300g** apple juice (clear)
- **150g** grape juice (white)
- **150g** water (cold)
- **15g** glycerin

**Dry:**
- **21g** erythritol
- **14g** xylitol
- **20g** waxy maize starch / Ultratex → 🟡 **20g** corn starch (heat apple juice to dissolve corn starch first; it doesn't cold-swell like Ultratex)
- **1g** citric acid (or ~15ml lemon juice)
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Fill to MAX:**
- Stevia drops (8 drops) → 🟡 pinch of sucralose (or use stevia drops if available)

**Optional:**
- **225g** pear juice instead of grape juice (reduce water accordingly)

**Steps:**
1. Heat apple juice in a saucepan. Whisk in corn starch until dissolved and slightly thickened. Let cool.
2. Add cooled apple juice mixture, grape juice, water, and glycerin to pint.
3. Combine remaining dry ingredients separately, then pour into pint and blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min.
4. Adjust sweetness with sucralose. Stir.
5. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
6. Sorbet setting.
7. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 437 kcal | 1g fat | 1g protein | 130g carbs

**PAC:** 30

### Hokkaido Pumpkin
Tags: pumpkin, cooked-base, low-fat, low-sugar, scoopable

**Prep (steam pumpkin):**
- **450g** Hokkaido pumpkin (or butternut squash)

**Wet:**
- **75g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- **16g** glycerin
- **16g** brandy or vodka 40%
- **16g** honey

**Dry:**
- **12g** erythritol
- **8g** xylitol
- **10g** soy protein isolate → 🟡 **10g** whey protein (no longer vegan)
- Salty Stability (5g):
  - **3.7g** inulin
  - **0.6g** soy lecithin
  - **0.3g** CMC
  - **0.2g** guar gum
  - **0.17g** salt
  - **0.07g** xanthan gum

**Fill to MAX:**
- **50g** cream 32%
- **~22g** soy milk (unsweetened)
- Flavor drops Vanilla (7 drops) → 🟡 **3g** vanilla extract

**Steps:**
1. Cut pumpkin into pieces. Steam in a saucepan with very little water, a pinch of salt, nutmeg, ginger, and turmeric. ~20-30 min until soft.
2. Let puree cool slightly. Add to pint with wet ingredients.
3. Blend until smooth.
4. Combine dry ingredients separately, then pour into pint and blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min until thickeners hydrate.
5. Add cream, remaining soy milk (~22g to MAX line), and vanilla extract. Stir.
6. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
7. Sorbet setting, scrape down, then Ice Cream setting.
8. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.

### 51. Ice Cream Stabilizer (ICSv2)

**Source:** ice-creamery repo — Ice Cream Stabilizer (ICS)

Pre-mixed stabilizer for Ninja Creami recipes. No swaps needed — all ingredients are in our pantry.

**Type:** Premix blend (not a standalone recipe)

**Dry:**
- **100g** erythritol
- **100g** inulin
- **10g** CMC
- **3.5g** guar gum
- **3.5g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Steps:**
1. Weigh and mix all dry ingredients in a jar with a secure lid and shake vigorously.
2. Store in an air-tight container with enough volume.

**Dose:** 30g for Deluxe (24oz), 20g for regular (16oz).

Per 30g dose: 13.8g erythritol, 13.8g inulin, 1.4g CMC, 0.5g guar, 0.5g salt, 0.14g xanthan.
Per 20g dose: 9.2g erythritol, 9.2g inulin, 0.9g CMC, 0.3g guar, 0.3g salt, 0.09g xanthan.

**Nutrition (per 30g dose):** 32 kcal | 0g fat | 0g protein | 29g carbs

### 52. Instamaze

**Source:** ice-creamery repo — Instamaze

Pre-mixed starchy stabilizer. **Draft / Work in Progress.**

> **Warning:** The original uses Ultratex (cold-swelling starch). Swapping to corn starch means this premix can NO LONGER be used cold — corn starch requires heat to activate. This fundamentally changes the "instant" nature of the blend. Consider skipping this premix and addressing stabilizer needs per-recipe instead.

**Type:** Premix blend (not a standalone recipe)

**Dry:**
- **160g** Waxy Maize Starch / Ultratex → 🟡 **160g** corn starch (no longer cold-swelling; must cook a slurry before use — loses the "instant" property)
- **110g** inulin
- **17g** GMS → 🟢 **17g** soy lecithin
- **6g** CMC
- **5g** salt
- **2g** xanthan gum

**Steps:**
1. Weigh and mix all dry ingredients in a jar with a secure lid and shake vigorously.
2. Store in an air-tight container.
3. To use: make a slurry with a portion of your recipe's liquid, heat to 85°C+ while stirring until thickened, cool, then add to base.

**Dose:** 25g for Deluxe (24oz), 17g for regular (16oz).

3-ingredient fallback (per 15g dose): 1.5g guar + 0.5g xanthan + 0.5g salt.

### 53. Irish Cream

**Source:** ice-creamery repo — Irish Cream (Deluxe)

Irish Cream ice cream with notes of vanilla, coffee, and chocolate. Spun on Lite Ice Cream, scrape down and re-mix.

**Prep:**
- **50ml**  water (almost boiling, 90°C)
- **15g** cocoa powder

**Wet:**
- **350ml**  soy milk (unsweetened)
- **70g** Irish whiskey 40%

**Dry:**
- **30g** skim milk powder (SMP)
- Salty Stability (15g):
  - **11.0g** inulin
  - **1.8g** soy lecithin
  - **0.9g** CMC
  - **0.6g** guar gum
  - **0.5g** salt
  - **0.2g** xanthan gum
- **4g** instant coffee
- **2g** vanilla bean powder → 🟡 **3g** vanilla extract (less complex aroma than whole bean)
- sweetener tablets (15 pcs, ≈60g sugar-equiv sweetness) → 🟡 **0.1g** sucralose (purely sweetness, no bulk — recipe doesn't need the tablet bulk)

**Fill to MAX:**
- **150ml**  cream 32%
- Flavor drops Vanilla (8 drops) → 🟡 **3g** vanilla extract

**Steps:**
1. Mix the hot water with cocoa powder to bloom it, then let it cool down.
2. Add wet ingredients and the bloomed cocoa to empty Creami tub.
3. Combine dry ingredients separately, then pour into tub and blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min.
4. Add cream and vanilla extract to MAX line. Stir.
5. For better results, let the base age in the fridge (covered) for a few hours or overnight.
6. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
7. Lite Ice Cream setting, scrape down, then Re-Spin or Mix-In.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 976 kcal | 56g fat | 29g protein | 41g carbs

**PAC:** 32

### 54. Jeni Britton's Base

**Source:** ice-creamery repo — Jeni Britton's Base (Deluxe)

Classic Jeni Britton Bauer ice cream base, scaled to Deluxe tub. Cooked base. Add any flavor to your liking.

**Prep:**
- **45ml**  milk 3.5%
- **10g** corn starch

**Wet:**
- **250ml**  milk 3.5%
- light corn syrup (20ml) → 🟡 20ml glucose syrup or rice syrup (slightly different sweetness profile)
- **40g** cream cheese 23%
- **225ml**  cream 32%

**Dry:**
- **95g** sugar
- **1g** salt

**Steps:**
1. Make cornstarch slurry: whisk corn starch with the 45ml milk until smooth.
2. In a saucepan, combine 250ml milk, glucose syrup, sugar, and salt. Bring to a gentle boil over medium heat.
3. Stir in the cornstarch slurry and simmer 1-2 min until slightly thickened.
4. Remove from heat and whisk in the softened cream cheese until fully incorporated.
5. Add cream, stir to combine.
6. Cool quickly (ice bath or fridge) until completely cold — ideally overnight.
7. Pour into pint. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
8. Ice Cream setting, scrape down, then Re-Spin if needed.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 1467 kcal | 91g fat | 18g protein | 146g carbs

**PAC:** 21

  - **7.3g** inulin
  - **1.2g** soy lecithin
  - **0.6g** CMC
  - **0.4g** guar gum
  - **0.3g** salt
  - **0.13g** xanthan gum
- **5g** Ultratex / Waxy Maize Starch → 🟡 **5g** corn starch *(optional; make a slurry with 30ml warm water, heat until thickened, cool, then add)*

4. Combine dry ingredients separately, then pour into tub and blend with immersion blender on full speed, 1-2 min.
5. Add corn starch slurry (if using) and water to MAX line. Stir.

### 65. Matcha Coconut

**Source:** ice-creamery repo — Matcha Coconut (Deluxe)

Matcha ice cream with coconut and soy milk. Cooked/heated base. Process on Sorbet, then Re-Spin or Mix-In.

**Wet:**
- **350ml**  soy milk (unsweetened)
- **100ml**  coconut milk 22% (canned)
- **15g** glycerin
- **15g** brandy or vodka 40%

**Dry:**
- **12g** matcha green tea powder
- SweEX (40g):
  - **24g** erythritol
  - **16g** xylitol
- Salty Stability (10g):
  - **7.3g** inulin
  - **1.2g** soy lecithin
  - **0.6g** CMC
  - **0.4g** guar gum
  - **0.3g** salt
  - **0.13g** xanthan gum
- **25g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **25g** whey protein
- **25g** skim milk powder (SMP)

**Fill to MAX:**
- **88ml**  soy milk (unsweetened)
- Flavor drops Vanilla (2 drops) → 🟡 **2g** vanilla extract

**Steps:**
1. Heat soy milk and coconut milk in a small pot to 80°C, take off heat.
2. Combine matcha and other dry ingredients in a small bowl.

4. Add glycerin and brandy. Pour into empty Creami tub.
5. Add remaining soy milk and vanilla extract to MAX line. Adjust sweetness.
6. Rest in fridge until fully cooled (a few hours or overnight).
7. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
8. Sorbet setting, scrape down, then Re-Spin or Mix-In.

### 71. PB Swirl

- **20g** PB powder → 🟡 **10g** peanut butter (higher fat, richer; stir until smooth)
- **10g** xylitol
- **0.25g** salt

**Steps:**
1. Put ingredients into a bowl and stir until smooth.
2. Make several small holes in your processed base using the blunt end of a chopstick (1 center + 6-8 in a circle).
3. Fill holes with the cream/PB mix.

### 80. Pistachio Ice Cream

**Source:** ice-creamery repo — Pistachio Ice Cream (Deluxe)

Pistachio ice cream with cottage cheese. Heated base. Process on Lite Ice Cream.

**Wet:**
- **400ml**  soy milk (unsweetened)
- **100g** cottage cheese 4%
- **30g** pistachio butter
- **20g** almond butter (creamy) *(optional: use more pistachio butter instead)*
- **20g** glycerin
- **15g** brandy or vodka 40% *(alternative: 12g additional glycerin for sober recipe)*

**Dry:**
- SweEX (35g):
  - **21g** erythritol
  - **14g** xylitol
- Salty Stability (15g):
  - **11.0g** inulin
  - **1.8g** soy lecithin
  - **0.9g** CMC
  - **0.6g** guar gum
  - **0.5g** salt
  - **0.2g** xanthan gum
- **15g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **15g** whey protein
- **15g** skim milk powder (SMP)
- **3g** GMS → 🟢 **3g** soy lecithin

1. Heat the soy milk to about 60°C.
2. Whisk in the dry ingredients (blend them together first).
3. Dissolve the nut butters in the warm milk, then add cottage cheese, glycerin, and brandy. Blend to emulsify.
4. Let blender run until thickeners are hydrated, 1-2 min.
5. Add cream and vanilla extract to MAX line. Stir.
6. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
7. Lite Ice Cream setting, scrape down, then Re-Spin if needed.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 895 kcal | 41g fat | 52g protein | 94g carbs

**PAC:** 31

### Protein Choc (Deluxe)
Tags: chocolate, hi-protein, low-sugar, scoopable

**Source:** ice-creamery — Protein Choc (Deluxe) · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Protein%20Choc%20(Deluxe))

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

Chocolate ice cream, very high in protein. Boost chocolate with cocoa or dark chocolate mix-ins.

**Prep (bloom cocoa — heat milk to >80°C):**
- **150g** Marata 1.5% milk
- **45g** cocoa powder
- SweEX (45g):
  - **27g** erythritol
  - **18g** xylitol

**Wet:**
- **300g** Marata 1.5% milk
- **20g** glycerin
- **15g** brandy or vodka 40%

**Dry:**
- **50g** chocolate whey protein → 🟢 **50g** whey protein + **2 drops** chocolate flavor drops *(already has 45g cocoa for flavor)*
- Salty Stability (15g):
  - **11.0g** inulin
  - **1.8g** soy lecithin
  - **0.9g** CMC
  - **0.6g** guar gum
  - **0.5g** salt
  - **0.2g** xanthan gum

**Fill to MAX:**
- **40ml** cream 32% → 🟡 **~40g** Marata 1.5% milk *(less rich; optional: keep cream for richer pint)*
- Flavor drops Vanilla (6 drops) → 🟡 **3g** vanilla extract

**Mix-ins:**
- **35g** sugar-free milk chocolate, frozen & chopped

**Steps:**
1. Heat 150g milk to >80°C in microwave. Add cocoa and sweetener. Blend to smooth paste.
2. Add bloomed cocoa paste and other wet ingredients to pint.
3. Mix dry ingredients separately.
4. Pour into tub and QUICKLY immersion blend on full speed.
5. Let blender run 1-2 min to hydrate thickeners.
6. Add remaining milk to MAX line and vanilla extract. Stir.
7. Rest in fridge a few hours or overnight.
8. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
9. Lite Ice Cream setting.
10. Scrape down sides with butter knife, push top down with spoon. Re-Spin if not creamy enough.
11. Make a hole to the bottom. Add chopped chocolate in layers. Mix-In.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 823 kcal | 28g fat | 65g protein | 98g carbs

**PAC:** 30

⚠️ GI check: Inulin = 11.0g (from SS). Xylitol = 18g. Gums = 0.9g CMC + 0.6g guar + 0.2g xanthan = 1.7g. Erythritol = 27g (4.0% of 680g). ALL SAFE.

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

**Draft · Cooked Base · Seasonal**

**Prep:**
- **180ml**  soy milk 1.6% *(or any ~2% milk)*
- **4g** pumpkin spice
- **3g** annatto seed powder *(optional, for color)*

**Wet:**
- **280ml**  soy milk 1.6% *(or any ~2% milk)*
- **15g** glycerin
- **8g** brandy or vodka 40% *(alternative: 6g additional glycerin)*

**Dry:**
- SweEX (40g):
  - **24g** erythritol
  - **16g** xylitol
- **15g** inulin
- **15g** skim milk powder (SMP)
- **15g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **15g** whey protein

- **0.5g** xanthan gum

### 84. Radler Zäpfle (Deluxe)

**Source:** ice-creamery — Radler Zäpfle (Deluxe) · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Radler%20Z%C3%A4pfle%20(Deluxe))

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

**Weird Science · ~~Vegan~~ (whey swap)**

A bitter-sweet sorbet from a typical German summer beer mix (radler/shandy). Also works with Berliner Weiße or Belgian Früli (strawberry beer — leave out VG).

**Wet:**
- **330ml**  radler / shandy beer ~2.4 vol% *(any brand)*
- **10g** glycerin
- **10ml**  lemon juice

**Dry:**
- **15g** inulin
- **15g** soy protein isolate → 🟡 **15g** whey protein *(no longer vegan/dairy-free)*
- **1g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Mix-ins:**
- **10g** salty pretzels *(~5 small pretzels, broken; +40 kcal, +7g carbs)*

**Optional:**
- Flavor drops Peach/Maracuja (3 drops) → 🟡 skip

**Steps:**
1. Pour beer into empty Creami tub, add glycerin and lemon juice.
2. Weigh and mix dry ingredients in a small bowl.
3. Vigorously whisk the dry into the wet.
4. Let defoam in the fridge for ~2 hours, give a final stir.
5. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
6. Sorbet setting, scrape down, then Mix-In with pretzels. Refreeze 1-2h before serving.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 245 kcal | 2g fat | 14g protein | 43g carbs

### 89. Salty Stability (Premix)

**Source:** ice-creamery — Salty Stability · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Salty%20Stability)

**Premix blend — yields 10 Deluxe doses (15g each) or 15 regular doses (10g each)**

**Dry:**
- **110g** inulin
- **18g** GMS → 🟢 **18g** soy lecithin
- **9g** CMC (carboxymethyl cellulose)
- **6g** guar gum
- **5g** salt
- **2g** xanthan gum

**Total:** 150g

**Directions:**
1. Take a large bowl (baking-size), measure all ingredients on a scale.
2. To get an even mix, start with 30g inulin, mix with all other ingredients, then progressively add more inulin while mixing.
3. Whisk carefully but thoroughly (ball whisk works best).
4. Store in an air-tight container with enough volume.

**Usage:**
- Use 15g per Deluxe tub (24 oz) or 10g per regular tub (16 oz).
- Drizzle mix into the vortex of a running blender, or add to mixing container and quickly integrate with immersion blender.
- Combine with any granulated sweetener or other dry ingredients before blending in.
- For optimal texture, heat part of the liquid (at least 200ml) to 50-80°C and blend the mix into the hot liquid.

**Quick substitute (per 15g dose):** 1.5g guar + 0.5g xanthan + 0.5g salt *(not as good)*

**Per 15g dose:** 11.0g inulin, 1.8g soy lecithin, 0.9g CMC, 0.6g guar, 0.5g salt, 0.2g xanthan

**Per 10g dose:** 7.3g inulin, 1.2g soy lecithin, 0.6g CMC, 0.4g guar, 0.33g salt, 0.13g xanthan

**Nutrition (per 15g dose):** 40 kcal | 0.5g fat | 0g protein | 13g carbs

**Nutrition (per pint):** 714 kcal | 29g fat | 32g protein | 68g carbs

**PAC:** 33

⚠️ GI check: Inulin = 15g + 10g (Ultratex swap) = 25g (under 30g). Xylitol = 6g. Gums = 0.3g xanthan = 0.3g. Erythritol = 9g + 8g = 17g (2.5% of 695g). ALL SAFE.

### 93. Starry Night (Deluxe)

**Source:** ice-creamery — Starry Night (Deluxe) · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Starry%20Night%20(Deluxe))

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

**Cooked Base**

Dark cacao base with white chocolate flakes mixed in. Macros include mix-ins.

**Wet:**
- **475ml**  soy milk 1.6% *(or any ~2% milk)*
- **20g** Cointreau 40 vol%
- **10g** glycerin

**Dry:**
- SweEX (45g):
  - **27g** erythritol
  - **18g** xylitol
- **25g** cocoa powder (intense/dark, 11% fat)
- **20g** inulin
- **20g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **20g** whey protein
- **15g** skim milk powder (SMP)
- Salty Stability (10g):
  - **7.3g** inulin
  - **1.2g** soy lecithin
  - **0.6g** CMC
  - **0.4g** guar gum
  - **0.3g** salt
  - **0.13g** xanthan gum
- **4g** instant coffee *(optional)*
- **1g** cinnamon (Ceylon) *(optional)*

**Fill to MAX:**
- **25ml**  cream 32%
- Flavor drops Vanilla (6 drops) → 🟡 **2g** vanilla extract

**Mix-ins:**
- **25g** white chocolate shavings *(pre-frozen; +140 kcal, +14g sugar)*
- **10g** almond slivers *(chopped; +59 kcal, +0.4g sugar)*

**Steps:**
1. Pre-freeze chocolate and almonds at least 1 hour before processing.
2. Heat the soy milk to ~60°C (first steam rising).
3. Whisk in pre-mixed dry ingredients.
4. Pour the cooled-down mix into empty Creami tub, add Cointreau, glycerin, blend to emulsify.
5. Add cream and vanilla extract. Stir.
6. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
7. Lite Ice Cream setting, scrape down, then Mix-In with pre-frozen chocolate and almonds.

**Nutrition (per pint, with mix-ins):** 852 kcal | 34g fat | 45g protein | 120g carbs

**PAC:** 30

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

**Favorite**

A great combination of tart flavors, based on quark/topfen. In Greece, use cream cheese as substitute.

**Wet:**
- **245g** quark / topfen 14.4% → 🟡 **245g** cream cheese (low-fat) *(or strained yogurt)*
- **200g** strawberries
- **125ml**  soy milk 1.6% *(or any ~2% milk)*
- **15g** brandy or vodka 40%
- **15g** glycerin
- **15ml**  lemon juice

**Dry:**
- SweEX (30g):
  - **18g** erythritol
  - **12g** xylitol
- **20g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **20g** whey protein
- Salty Stability (10g):
  - **7.3g** inulin
  - **1.2g** soy lecithin
  - **0.6g** CMC
  - **0.4g** guar gum
  - **0.3g** salt
  - **0.13g** xanthan gum
- **2g** beet root powder *(optional, for color)*
- **1g** salt

3. Pour into tub and QUICKLY immersion blend on full speed.

6. Optionally, let base age in fridge for a few hours or overnight.
7. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
8. Lite Ice Cream setting (or Frozen Yogurt), scrape down, then Mix-In.

### 102. Topfenpalatschinken (Deluxe)

**Source:** ice-creamery — Topfenpalatschinken (Deluxe) · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Topfenpalatschinken%20(Deluxe))

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

**Draft**

Classic Austrian dessert reimagined as ice cream: quark/lime base with rum-soaked raisins and pancake ribbons as mix-ins.

**Prep (pancakes):**
- **125ml**  soy milk 1.6% *(or any low-fat milk)*
- **75g** flour (cake flour)
- **60g** egg *(1 medium)*
- pinch of salt
- **15ml**  avocado oil *(to fry)*

**Wet:**
- **245g** quark / topfen 14.4% → 🟡 **245g** cream cheese (low-fat) *(or strained yogurt)*
- **100ml**  soy milk 1.6% *(or any low-fat milk)*
- **20ml**  lime juice + zest (organic)
- **10g** glycerin

**Dry:**
- SweEX (35g):
  - **21g** erythritol
  - **14g** xylitol
- Salty Stability (5g):
  - **3.7g** inulin
  - **0.6g** soy lecithin
  - **0.3g** CMC
  - **0.2g** guar gum
  - **0.17g** salt
  - **0.07g** xanthan gum
- **2g** vanilla bean powder → 🟡 **2g** vanilla extract *(add with wet)*
- **1g** salt

**Mix-ins:**
- **15g** sultanas *(coarsely chopped, soaked overnight in rum; +45 kcal, +10g sugar)*
- **15g** Jamaica rum 43% *(for soaking raisins)*
- **~160g** pancake ribbons *(as prepared from Prep)*

**Steps:**
1. Bake pancakes from prep ingredients in a pan with a little oil. Let cool, cut into ribbons, refrigerate.
2. Chop raisins, cover with rum in a container, chill overnight.
3. Zest the lime first, then juice it.
4. Add wet ingredients (including vanilla extract) to empty Creami tub.
5. Mix dry ingredients together.
6. Pour into tub and QUICKLY immersion blend on full speed.
7. Let blender run 1-2 min to hydrate thickeners.
8. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
9. Lite Ice Cream setting, scrape down, then Mix-In with drained raisins and pancake ribbons.

**Nutrition (per pint, with mix-ins):** 1171 kcal | 60g fat | 45g protein | 124g carbs

**PAC:** 32

### 104. Tropical Mudslide (Deluxe)

**Source:** ice-creamery — Tropical Mudslide (Deluxe) · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Tropical%20Mudslide%20(Deluxe))

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

**Draft · Boozy · Layered (2 flavors)**

A boozy combination of layered chocolate and coconut ice cream. Prepared in 3 phases, combined to 2 layers. Do not overprocess, to keep discernible layers.

**Prep (chocolate layer):**
- **100ml**  water (boiling)
- **20g** cocoa powder 11%
- **20g** dark chocolate 70%
- **50ml**  almond liqueur (Amaretto) 21.5 vol%

**Prep (coconut layer):**
- **100ml**  soy milk 1.6% *(heat to 90°C)*
- **20g** coconut flakes (desiccated) *(soak in hot milk for 30-60 min)*
- Batida de Côco (50ml) → 🟡 50ml Malibu (or any coconut liqueur)

**Wet:**
- **225ml**  soy milk 1.6%
- **50g** cottage cheese 4%
- **25g** Jamaica rum 43%

**Dry:**
- **5g** xylitol
- **20g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **20g** whey protein
- ICSv2 (20g):
  - **9.2g** erythritol
  - **9.2g** inulin
  - **0.92g** CMC
  - **0.32g** guar gum
  - **0.32g** salt
  - **0.09g** xanthan gum
- **3g** vanilla bean powder → 🟡 **3g** vanilla extract *(add with wet)*

**Fill to MAX:**
- Flavor drops Vanilla (7 drops per half) → 🟡 **2g** vanilla extract per half

**Steps:**
1. Bloom cocoa with boiling water, melt chocolate into paste, add Amaretto.
2. Heat 100ml soy milk to 90°C, soak coconut flakes 30-60 min. When cooled, add Malibu.
3. Add wet ingredients to empty Creami tub.
4. Mix dry ingredients together.
5. Pour into tub and QUICKLY immersion blend on full speed. Let run 1-2 min.
6. Pour half the base into the coconut bowl, stir, add vanilla extract, refrigerate.
7. Pour chocolate mix into the remaining half, blend, add vanilla extract, freeze 4 hours.
8. After 4h, gently pour the coconut mix on top of the frozen chocolate layer.
9. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
10. Sorbet setting, scrape down, then Mix-In. Do not overprocess.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 879 kcal | 33g fat | 42g protein | 66g carbs

**PAC:** 32

---

### 105. Tropical Rum Punch (Deluxe)

**Source:** ice-creamery — Tropical Rum Punch (Deluxe) · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Tropical%20Rum%20Punch%20(Deluxe))

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

**Cooked Base · Vegan · Dairy-Free**

Blends pineapple, orange, and mango juices with coconut milk and a touch of Jamaica Rum.

**Prep (heated):**
- **200ml**  juice (60% orange / 20% mango / 20% pineapple)
- **70ml**  water *(reduced from 100ml to offset canned coconut milk)*
- **30ml**  lime or lemon juice
- **15g** pomegranate syrup (grenadine / nar ekşisi)

**Wet:**
- **225g** pineapple in juice (canned) *(1 can ~227g, 140g fruit)*
- **45g** Jamaica rum 43%

**Dry:**
- **10g** banana powder / green plantain → 🟡 **10g** corn starch *(recipe heats to 90°C, so starch activates fine)*
- **20g** coconut milk powder → 🟡 50ml canned coconut milk (~18%) *(add to prep liquid)*
- **20g** inulin
- **1g** salt
- **1g** xanthan gum

**Adjust sweetness:**
- Flavor drops Peach/Maracuja (9 drops) → 🟡 skip

**Steps:**
1. In a saucepan, heat prep ingredients + canned coconut milk to ~90°C.
2. Mix dry ingredients (corn starch, inulin, salt, xanthan) and whisk into the hot liquid.
3. Let cool, then blend into the pineapple and rum.
4. Stir well.
5. Optionally, let base age in fridge for a few hours or overnight.
6. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
7. Sorbet setting, scrape down, then Re-Spin.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 647 kcal | 11g fat | 4g protein | 106g carbs

**PAC:** 33

**Cooked Prep**

A balanced, creamy frozen yogurt — light on sugar, rich in texture. Versatile base for any topping or swirl.

- **1g** guar gum
- **1g** salt

**Adjust sweetness:**
- Flavor drops Vanilla (3 drops) → 🟡 **1g** vanilla extract

**Mix-ins:**
- **50g** no-sugar-added cherry jam *(manual mix-in; +30 kcal, +3.6g sugar)*

**Optional:**
- **3ml**  vanilla extract *(add before freezing)*

**Steps:**
1. In a small saucepan, mix all prep ingredients and heat to 90°C on medium while whisking.
2. Add yogurt and the milk preparation to empty Creami tub.
3. Mix dry ingredients together.
4. Pour into tub and QUICKLY immersion blend on full speed.
5. Let blender run 1-2 min to hydrate thickeners.
6. Add vanilla extract. Stir.
7. Optionally, let base age in fridge for a few hours or overnight.
8. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
9. Frozen Yogurt setting, scrape down, then Mix-In with jam.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 623 kcal | 25g fat | 34g protein | 72g carbs

**PAC:** 31

### 109. Vanilla Paste (Condiment)

**Source:** ice-creamery — Vanilla Paste · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Vanilla%20Paste)

**Not a spin recipe — homemade vanilla paste for ice cream making**

**Draft**

Superior to store-bought vanilla extracts. Use grade B vanilla beans (preferably with residual moisture). Sterilize all equipment for long shelf life (months, refrigerated).

**Prep:**
- **35g** vanilla beans (Bourbon) *(17-18 beans, grade B or A, 12-13 cm)*

**Wet:**
- **140ml**  water (cold)
- **24ml**  agave syrup

**Dry:**
- **80g** trehalose → 🟡 **80g** sugar *(original suggests table sugar as alternative; slightly sweeter than trehalose)*
- **1g** citric acid

**Steps:**
1. Split and chop vanilla beans, removing hard ends.
2. Heat water, sugar, and agave syrup on medium-low until dissolved.
3. Add chopped beans, simmer on low ~45 min until reduced to thick consistency.
4. Cool, then blend thoroughly in food processor.
5. Strain to capture all flavor and seeds.
6. Store in a jar in the refrigerator. Paste will thicken further as it chills.

*Don't waste the sieve residue — dry it and grind to powder for breakfast bowls.*

**Nutrition (per tsp / 5g):** 7 kcal | 0g fat | 0g protein | 2g carbs

### 114. Vegan Lime Light (Deluxe)

**Source:** ice-creamery — Vegan Lime Light (Deluxe) · [README](https://github.com/jhermann/ice-creamery/tree/main/recipes/Vegan%20Lime%20Light%20(Deluxe))

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

Refreshing dairy-free lime ice cream with tofu and soy milk. Zesty and creamy.

**Prep:**
- **50ml**  Limoncello 28%
- SweEX (45g):
  - **27g** erythritol
  - **18g** xylitol
- **20ml**  lime juice + zest (organic) *(1 lime; optionally 2 limes or 1 lemon)*

**Wet:**
- **200ml**  soy milk 1.6% *(heat and blend with the tofu)*
- **90g** tofu (plain) *(silken tofu preferred)*
- **250ml**  soy milk 1.6%

**Dry:**
- Salty Stability (15g):
  - **11.0g** inulin
  - **1.8g** soy lecithin
  - **0.9g** CMC
  - **0.6g** guar gum
  - **0.5g** salt
  - **0.2g** xanthan gum
- **10g** Waxy Maize Starch / Ultratex → 🟡 **10g** corn starch *(optional, for viscosity; dissolve into the hot soy milk when softening tofu)*

**Fill to MAX:**
- Flavor drops "Natural" stevia (12 drops) → 🟡 **~0.02g** pinch sucralose

**Steps:**
1. Zest the lime(s), rub zest into the SweEX, add juice and Limoncello. Set aside.
2. Microwave 200ml soy milk with tofu to soften. Blend with stabilizer mix (and corn starch) until smooth.
3. Add the rest of the soy milk and the prepared zest/sweetener mix.
4. Immersion blend on full speed to homogenize.
5. Add sucralose. Stir.
6. Age base in fridge (covered) for a few hours or overnight.
7. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
8. Lite Ice Cream setting, scrape down, Re-Spin.

**Nutrition (per pint):** 515 kcal | 15g fat | 25g protein | 89g carbs

**PAC:** 32

Velvety nuts, sweetened with dates, kissed by vanilla. Medjool dates give a natural caramel note.

- **10g** brandy or vodka 40%

5. Mix dry ingredients together.
6. Pour into tub and QUICKLY immersion blend on full speed.

9. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.

**Ninja Creami Deluxe [24 oz]**

**Seasonal**

Almond butter + Amaretto ice cream with buttermilk base. Winter mix-ins: gingerbread, dark chocolate, almonds, raisins. Macros include mix-ins.

Soaked raisins variant (option 2): soak 40g chopped raisins in 45g rum (43%) a day before, drain surplus rum into base (~26g), leave out Amaretto.

- **2g** GMS → 🟢 **2g** soy lecithin

**Wet:**
- **300ml**  buttermilk 1%
- **40ml**  amaretto 28% *(with option 2: replace with 26g surplus rum)*

  - **1.8g** soy lecithin

  - **0.6g** guar gum
  - **0.5g** salt
  - **0.2g** xanthan gum

- **40g** Option 2: sultanas *(chopped, soaked in rum; +119 kcal, +27g sugar)* · 45g Jamaica rum 43% *(drain raisins, add surplus to base)*

**Wet:**

- **10g** molasses

**Dry:**
- **30g** trehalose → 🟡 **30g** sugar *(original suggests sugar as alternative; adjust stevia accordingly)*
- **15g** inulin
- **15g** ube yam root powder *(alt: blueberry or lingonberry powder)*
- **15g** whey + casein protein → 🟢 **15g** whey protein
- **10g** Waxy Maize Starch / Ultratex → 🟡 **10g** corn starch *(cold base — heat 100ml of the soy milk to 90°C, whisk in corn starch until thickened, let cool, then add to tub)*
- **3g** beet root powder *(optional, for color)*
- **0.75g** salt
- **0.75g** xanthan gum

**Fill to MAX:**
- prepared grappa/lime/syrup mix *(blend into base before tasting)*
- **60ml**  cream 32%
- stevia drops "natural" (15 drops) → 🟡 **~0.03g** pinch sucralose

**Mix-ins:**
- **20g** cranberries (sugared, chopped) *(soak overnight in some of the grappa; +64 kcal, +12g sugar)*

**Steps:**
1. Chop cranberries, soak overnight in some of the grappa (covered, in fridge).
2. Zest and juice the lime, mix with syrup, remaining grappa, and raspberry emulsion.
3. Heat 100ml of the soy milk to 90°C, whisk in corn starch until thickened. Let cool.
4. Add remaining wet ingredients and cooled starch paste to empty Creami tub.
5. Mix remaining dry ingredients together.
6. Pour into tub and QUICKLY immersion blend on full speed.
7. Let blender run 1-2 min to hydrate thickeners.
8. Add prepared grappa/lime mix, cream, and sucralose to MAX line. Stir.
9. Freeze 24h with lid on. Flatten any humps.
10. Lite Ice Cream setting, scrape down, then Mix-In with drained cranberries.

**Nutrition (per pint, base only):** 819 kcal | 27g fat | 28g protein | 94g carbs

**PAC:** 32
